  Contract No:          F – 20 Well Drilling Program                  Page 156 of 181

        legislative and regulatory environmental requirements that occur during the performance of the
    WORK. Without relieving COMPANY of any of its obligations under Item 3.2, if COMPANY
      does not promptly proceed to perform its obligations under this Item 3, PPL/PEDEC may take
        part to any degree it deems necessary in the control and removal of any pollution, dumping,
        spillage or contamination which is the responsibility of COMPANY under this Item 3.2, or
      which is due to any act or omission of COMPANY. COMPANY shall reimburse PPL/PEDEC
        for costs arising from such actions of PPL/PEDEC upon receipt of invoice from PPL/PEDEC.
     PPL/PEDEC shall have the right to deduct such costs from any sum due or becoming due to
    COMPANY under the CONTRACT.
   COMPANY shall operate a management system that is able to demonstrate compliance with
       environmental laws, rules and regulations applicable to its activities, products and services.
   MSA/OEOC  and  its  subcontractors  shall  take  all  necessary  steps  to  ensure  that  the
       environmental impacts of their work are responsibly managed and shall take all necessary
      measures to ensure that:
   COMPANY’s personnel are aware of the environmental impact of the COMPANY’s operations
      and are sufficiently trained and competent to perform the WORK.
   COMPANY’s personnel operate systems, which will ensure that significant environmental
       impacts of WORK are identified and managed in accordance with a commitment to legal
      compliance and continual improvement.
   The environmental risks of operations associated with the WORK are evaluated and appropriate
       actions are taken to prevent accidents and reduce pollution, and that contingency plans are in
       place in the event of an incident.
   COMPANY will have adequate processes for monitoring the environmental performance of
        their WORK.
   COMPANY will, as a minimum, conduct the WORK and provide personnel, equipment,
         facilities and services consistent with IADC Environmental Guidelines. COMPANY’s proposals
        for environmental protection should be detailed in the QHSE Plan. This should include, but not
      be limited to:
  procedures and precautions to minimize damage to crops and vegetation, the local ecology and
   disturbance of wildlife and farm animals;
  recycling and waste disposal;
  Clean-up and restoration of seismic lines, Rig sites access roads and trails, fixed sites occupied
   during the survey.
   An ecological team shall be set up by COMPANY, with the purpose of collecting all waste,
       rubbish, food scraps, office waste and other discarded material from camps, created by the
    WORK. Such activities shall be performed on a daily basis. Such material shall be appropriately
       disposed of, according to PPL/PEDEC approved methods or existing methods established by
     COMPANY, which are proven to be acceptable to PPL/PEDEC. Well location and camp site
        shall be delivered to PPL/PEDEC without any waste, rubbish, food scraps, office waste and
       other discarded material. PPL/PEDEC has the right to hold Completion certificate till cleaning
       the well location and camp site by COMPANY.
   The access road and well location construction shall be accorded with construction program of
       well location. It minimizes any impacts on the local residents, prevent all soil contamination,
       prevent all ground water contamination, and prevent all surface water contamination. Cutting

       and waste water pit shall be lining and drainage system shall be good and to avoid farm and
        season canal contamination.
    Management regulation for transportation truck, drilling operation machine and tools shall be
         carried out strictly to avoid damage farm and trees, etc.
    Well site shall be flat and clean. All the equipment, materials, drilling tools shall be put in order.
    Control the rig noise to decrease the influence to surroundings while drilling at the inhabitation
         area.
    COMPANY shall perform on site segregation of wastes prior to transfer to the transfer area

    Ferrous and non-ferrous metals, plastics, and wood wastes, PETs and potentially paper and glass
    Materials shall be separately segregated and provision may be made for size reduction and
         containerization equipment.
    The COMPANY/Generators are encouraged to seek for recycle/re-use alternatives based on the
        Local available facilities, for final disposal of these materials.
    It should be noted that landfill disposal of metal waste materials and wood wastes shall be
         prohibited. For wood wastes re-use of and/or salvage of commercial timber, or deliver/release to
    local community are recommended.

Hazardous Wastes:
    Hazardous Waste, such as clinic waste, oil waste, waste produced by painting, insulation works
         shall be duly segregated.

Facilities of environment protection supplied to well site:
    Mud purifying circulation system
    Cooling water spraying and circulating system for drilling pump
    Special recovery tank for deposed oil
    Reserve house for valuable mud chemicals
    Automatic mud weighting supply system
    BOP stack is equipped according to drilling design
    Define a separation area for gathering all waste according to waste management procedure.
    Requirement of environment protection on location Sewage pit Area of the sewage pit: 1500
      m2
    Prior to spud mud pit and sewage pit must comply with the standard. Install cooling water
        spraying and circulating system for drilling pump and sewage circulating system for cleaning rig
         floor and equipment.
    Install BOP stack and pressure test shall be operated according to the drilling design to avoid
        blowout.
    Install oil supply lines and check whether there is any leakage.

Management of environment protection on location:
    In drilling operation keep mud and sewage from overflowing. If it happens, clean up timely.

────────────────────────────────────────────
────────────────────────────────────────────
          South Azadegan Oilfield Project

  Contract No:          F – 20 Well Drilling Program                  Page 158 of 181

   Mud chemicals shall be stored according to the standard and shall not be scattered around well
         site. If mud chemicals scatter while loading or unloading, clean it up at once. The sack of mud
       chemicals shall be recovered. Do not dispose it on site.
   Enhance water management at well location and do not wash equipment with fresh water. Stop
       water flowing for long time. Overhead tanks shall be utilized.
   Oil management shall be carefully at the well site. No oil dirt on site, no dripping and leaking at
        oil tank and manifold system area.
   After the well is completed, reinforce the sewage pit ，solidify the disposed mud and have
      environment procedure fulfilled.
   Install fuel supply lines and keep them in good condition.
   All the drilling fluid materials shall be recovered and shall not be left at the well site after
       completion.
   The environmental protection personnel from COMPANY and operator side shall check and
       accept the well site environment according to the regulation after completion. Only well site
      environment is qualified, the well can be delivered.
   Transition Area for Hazardous Waste shall also be provided by each COMPANY in his
       allocated area,
   PPL/PEDEC shall approve each area before use as well. The transition area of hazardous waste
        shall be:
  Paved, fenced, marked and illuminated
  Equipped with appropriate Fire Extinguisher and other emergency response equipment (e.g.,
  Eye-Wash and Emergency Shower, appropriate First Aid kits, neutralizer substances etc.).
  In compliance with the requirements of handling, transportation and storage of hazardous waste
   (based on usage chemical MSDS).
  Configured to accommodate a safe physical separation of incompatible waste materials (e.g.,
   acids, and alkalis, organic materials and strong oxidizing agents).
  Capable enough to provide storage for the entire range of hazardous wastes generated during the
   work.
 The area shall be provided with Spill Containment and means of preventing run off to and from
   the area.
  All waste containers to be stored shall be appropriately labeled.
  Hazardous Waste shall be transferred in a timely manner, within a maximum period of 48 hours,
   for any period in excess of this permission shall be granted from PPL/PEDEC.

   Standard of utilization and discharging of wastes on location
   Conduct drilling operation according to the design, drilling mud is needed before spud.
   Sewage that cannot be recovered shall be put into the pit， do not discharge
   Make full use of waste water. Water in mud pump and water brake must be utilized by
        circulating. Water that is used to wash drill floor, shall be recovered after deposition. The
       recovery rate of waste water shall reach 85％.
   Disposed oil shall be recovered entirely. Recovery rate shall be 100％. Dumping of waste oil
        shall be prohibited.
   In drilling operation, non-chrome thinner high temperature mud system shall be used often if
       engineering is qualified.
   While drilling in special area adopt mud creation inhabitation techniques to decrease the waste
     mud creation.

────────────────────────────────────────────
────────────────────────────────────────────
           South Azadegan Oilfield Project

   Contract No:          F – 20 Well Drilling Program                  Page 159 of 181

    After completion, all the mud chemicals, bentonite, clays shall be recovered and shall not be left
          at the well site. Mud in purifying tank shall also be recovered after completion.
    In case of any flammable or noxious gas encountered, steps shall be taken at once to prevent
        kicks and blowout. Gas shall be guided into ignition equipment to burn it away.
    Burning material that may result in serious smog or horrible smell at the well site is not allowed.
        For cement or barite that may result in granular dust pollution, operation shall be performed in a
        closed system.
    For abandoned well, filled with cement plugs to seal the oil, gas or water formation.
    COMPANY/Generators are required to transport all generated wastes to the location/facilities
         designated, and licensed by  authorities and/or approved/verified by the PPL/PEDEC  (as
         appropriate).
    All wastes shall be transferred by approved Sub‐ COMPANY (which could be a 3rd party Sub-
     COMPANY of the COMPANY/Generator itself). The Waste Transporter and any other waste
       management service COMPANY shall be licensed in accordance with local regulations and are
         in compliance with such  regulations. PPL/PEDEC  shall  verify and approve  the Waste
        Transportation COMPANY by checking licenses and qualifications, audit and inspections,
         verification of human resources and received trainings etc.
    The transportation COMPANY shall ensure that  all wastes are transported in a safe and
         responsible manner.

 12.4 Security

As per PPL/PEDEC as main client and MSA/OEOC agreed plan.
    Security for the WORK shall be arranged and administered by the MSA/OEOC. MSA/OEOC
         shall be fully advised in writing covering all aspects of security upon. Company shall liaise with
      PPL/PEDEC QHSE to develop operational and contingency plans prior to Mobilization.
    The Security guards shall be appropriately housed and provided for. Transport for the Security
        guards to perform field activities shall be made available according to standard Iranian Oilfield
        procedures.
    All MSA/OEOC’s personnel involved in the WORK shall possess the necessary security
        clearance documentation required by the Iranian authorities to work within the Operating Area.

 12.5 Quality

   1. Ensure that relevant experiences from the appraisal and production wells (if any) are captured and
     included in the operation programs /procedures.
   2. Check lists to be prepared for all equipment, available for PPL/PEDEC prior to start of operations.
   3. Procedures planned for all operations, available for PPL/PEDEC prior to start operations.
   4. High focus and follow up of KPIs (Key Performance Indicators) by NIOC procedures.
   5. Drilling risk assessment.
   6. All goods and equipment shall be checked as per engineering design and inspection certificates,
    PPL/PEDEC approval shall be obtained before running any goods in hole.
   7. Routine reports shall be submitted to PPL/PEDEC and any commented shall be corrected and
     informed engineering team.

           South Azadegan Oilfield Project

 12.6    Meetings

HSE meetings shall be convened on a monthly basis with an agenda to discuss the general issues about

HSE, any change in the contractor, in the nature and risk of the activities, HSE training program, safety

analysis of the activities, as well as investigation and analysis of the accidents. The Contractor’s site

manager and HSE Representative shall attend such meetings and report to the Client’s site manager and

HSE representative.

Weekly HSE meeting shall be conducted with the presence of MSA/OEOC HSE & Rig Contractor HSE
Supervisor.

12.8    Well Control

  1. Installation Requirements of BOP Stack and Control System:

          The distance between BOP control lines and relief lines shall be not less than 1m. Steel Gap Bridge should
        be placed in such a way to protect lines from damage by vehicle crossing. Sundries shall not be packed on the
         control line shield, on which welding operations are forbidden.

          The air supply shall be separated from that of driller’s console and directly connected to the main air supply
        system, and shall be equipped with liquid/gas separators. Excessive bending and pinching on air hoses are
         forbidden.

            Electric power to BOP control system shall be directly from the main switch of the power board, and
         controlled by independent switches.

          Accumulators shall be in good condition, charged at its rated value, and ready for work at any time.

  2. Installation Requirements of Choke Lines and Kill Lines, and Relief Lines:

      a)            Beside the drilling spool, one manual and one HCR gate valves on the side of chock manifold and two
       manual gate valves on the side of kill manifold shall be equipped, and the manual gate valve close to the spool shall
        be always open.

     b)           The high-pressure co-flex hose shall be installed as straight as possible and fixed by anchor after
          installation.

      c)              Relief lines (burning line and bleed line):

          No field welding is permitted on relief lines and the connecting style of relief lines must be flanged.
           The arrangement of relief lines shall take wind directions, dwellings, roads, oil tank areas, power lines and
         other facilities into consideration.

────────────────────────────────────────────
────────────────────────────────────────────
           South Azadegan Oilfield Project

   Contract No:          F – 20 Well Drilling Program                  Page 165 of 181

             While the two lines go in the same trend, the distance between the lines shall be at least 0.3m, and the two
           lines shall be fixed independently.
            The outlets of bleed line connecting with choke manifold shall be in a safe area which is at least 300m.
        The bleed line on the kill manifold side connecting with drilling spool shall be prepared and connected when
          necessary (about 70m from the wellhead).
            At every 10m~15m, turning points, and outlets, the lines shall be supported and fixed by cement
          foundations with anchor bolts or ground anchors. Hanging portions shall be supported firmly. If crossing the
          stumbling blocks such as brooks or ponds is needed, metal bridging supports are necessary.
              Pre-buried anchor bolts for cement foundations shall not be less than 20mm or 3/4" in diameter, and
           greater than 0.5m in length.

       3. Pressure test value and requirements:

       a) A pressure test shall be conducted successfully where there is no pressure decline in 5 minutes for a low-pressure
            test and a minimum of 10 minutes for a high-pressure test.

       b) Prior to each high-pressure test, a low-pressure test with 200-300 psi shall be carried out to verify sealing capability
            at the circumstance of low pressure and avoid washing out sealing elements.

       c) High pressure test should be to the rated working pressure of ram preventers or the rated working pressure of the
          wellhead, whichever is the lowest, or to the rating required in DRILLING ROGRAM. Variable bore rams should be
            initially pressure tested on the largest and smallest OD pipe sizes that may be used during the OPERATION.
         Annular preventers, with a joint of drill pipe installed, should be tested to 70% of their working pressure. The high
            test for Kelly cocks, kill lines and choke lines should be to their rated working pressures, each part in the choke
         manifold is tested respectively to its rated working pressures, the relief lines be pressure tested to no less than
          1,450psi (10Mpa).

       d) When casing cup tester is used, the above test pressure should not exceed 80% of the internal yield pressure (burst
           pressure) of the exposed casing.

       e) The control system of BOP shall be pressure tested to 3,000psi (21Mpa) for its reliability before putting into use.

         f) Except that the BOP control system is tested with hydraulic oil, other well control equipment shall be pressure tested
         by clear water.

       g)  Testing tools

            All required sizes of the plug type testers, and cup type testers should be available for pressure testing
          purpose.

          When the cup type tester(s) is used for pressure test, the connection of the cup testers has to be checked, to
          ensure it will stand the load during the high pressures test. For surface casing, the cup tester is to be set in middle of
           the first casing joint in the top, so the first casing connection (between wellhead and casing head housing) below
           the well head is to be tested too while testing BOP and wellhead.

   4. Pressure test frequency:

All ram type preventers, annular BOP, valves, choke and kill lines, choke manifold, Kelly valves and safety valves should be
pressure tested at the following frequencies:

              Prior to installation where possible.

────────────────────────────────────────────
────────────────────────────────────────────
          South Azadegan Oilfield Project

Contract No:          F – 20 Well Drilling Program                  Page 166 of 181

            After installation of wellhead and BOP assembly and prior to drilling.

            After the disconnection or repair of any pressure containment seal in the BOP stack, choke line, or choke
       manifold, but limited to the affected component.

            Prior to drilling into a confirmed or suspected high-pressure zone.

          At any time requested by the COMPANY REPRESENTATIVE.

            In any case, every one month or during the first trip after the one-month interval with a maximum interval
       of 45 days. This frequency is not required for shear rams. As a minimum, the shear rams are to be tested prior to
      resume drilling operation after 9-5/8” casing string has been set and adequate WOC.

5. Work Requirement of Well Control Equipment:

               With shut-in casing pressure lower than 1000psi, while snubbing with annular preventer, drill tools
        shall have 18-deg shoulder tool joints, and the snubbing velocity shall not be greater than 0.2m/s.

                 After shut off the annular preventer or ram preventer, with the shut-in casing pressure not greater than
       2000psi, drill tools are allowed to pull up and down with the velocity less than 0.2m/s, however, rotating or tool
        joint passing through are forbidden.

               While drilling through pay zone and thereafter, BOP function (close-open) test for the BOPs shall be
      performed regularly.

                  Kill manifolds shall not be used for drilling fluid fill-in.

              The maximum allowed shut-in casing pressure shall be remarkably indicated on choke manifolds.

                 All gate valves of well control manifolds shall be tagged with serial numbers, and be indicated with
       on/Off.

                 In case shear rams were used, after shearing the drill pipe with shear rams, retrieve the upper sheared
         drill pipe, lower a drill pipe joint with TIW valve installed into the hole and close pipe rams on it to prevent the
        possibility of shear ram’s failure.

6. Management of Well Control Equipment:

            Well control equipment used by the drilling crew shall be managed and operated by designated personnel
      with clear post responsibilities.

            Well site shall have specified component storeroom and rubber element storeroom with air conditioners
       respectively. The temperature of storerooms shall meet storage requirements of components and rubber elements.

            At least 1 set is available as back-up for every type ram assembly in the well site. Ram preventer side
      door seals and ram rubber, every type gasket and bolt etc. should be enough for backup.

7. Killing Method and Shut-in Method:

                Driller's method is recommended for well killing.

           The hard Shut-in method is recommended for shutting in the well in Fahliyan reservoir.

────────────────────────────────────────────
────────────────────────────────────────────
           South Azadegan Oilfield Project

   Contract No:          F – 20 Well Drilling Program                  Page 167 of 181

   8. Well Control during drilling:

       a)           Shut in Procedure in While on Bottom
                Position Kelly (if in use) and tool joint so that tool joints are clear of sealing elements.
               Shut off pumps, check for flow.
                   If flow is noted, close the annular preventer and open remote-controlled choke line valve (HCR).
                   If the well has been closed using the annular preventer, check space out (pipe rams vs. toll joint relative
           position) and close pipe rams and ram locks
              Bleed off pressure between pipe rams and annular preventer (if possible).
              Record shut-in drill pipe and casing pressures.
              Record volume gain and mark pits.
             Weigh mud in suction pits.
              Check blowout preventer and manifold for leaks.
              Check flowline and choke exhaust for flow.
              Check accumulator pressure.

       b)           Shut in Procedure in While Tripping
                   If a flow is detected, install full opening safety valve and close same.
                Position tool joints clear of the sealing elements and close annular preventer and open remote-controlled
         choke line valve (HCR).
             Make up grey valve (dart type inside BOP on full open safety valve). Then Make up Kelly or top drive
            (insert a pup joint or single between safety valve and top drive) and open safety valve.
              Record casing pressure.
              Check blowout preventer, stack, and manifold for leaks.
              Check valves for correct position.
              Check accumulator pressure.
              Begin stripping to bottom.

       c)           Shut in Procedure for Empty Wells
              Try to RIH with drill pipe as more as possible if hole condition permits, and the drill pipe must be
           installed a Kelly cock or safety valve.
               Close the annular preventer and open remote-controlled choke line valve (HCR).
                   If the well has been closed using the annular preventer, check space out (pipe rams vs. tool joint relative
           position) and close pipe rams and ram locks
              Bleed off pressure between pipe rams and annular preventer (jf possible).
              Record casing pressure

   9. Well Control During Running Casing:

The well control while running casing is important and critical, best practices shall be followed, including but not limited to:
              Change out pipe rams to casing rams prior to running casing into hydrocarbon bearing formation, if
           possible.
             The crossover between casing and drill pipe has to be available, and good practice is to make it up with a
            joint of DP and keep on V-door.

   10. Shut in Procedure During Logging:

────────────────────────────────────────────
────────────────────────────────────────────
           South Azadegan Oilfield Project

   Contract No:          F – 20 Well Drilling Program                  Page 168 of 181

Cable shall be pull out of the hole immediately when overflow is found during logging. If pit gain exceeds the specified
amount, the cable shall be cut immediately and treat the overflow in accordance with the shut-in procedure for empty wells.
Continuing to pull the cable through the closed annular preventer is not recommended.

   11. Well Control During Testing:
            No difference to while DRILLING OPERATION, since either there is testing string in the well (same as
          Shut-in procedure while on bottom), or tripping the string. (Same as shut-in procedure while tripping).
               Likely there is some crude in the testing string, and precaution has to be taken while circulating the crude
          out of hole during killing operations to ensure it is properly handled without causing environmental impact.
             To minimize the formation damage, the killing fluids used shall be weighted brine if possible, however
           in well control case when it occurs, killing well safely will take priority rather than protecting formation from
        damage on selection of killing fluids.

   12. Well Control During Stimulation:
                Similar to during testing.
               For any residual or alive acid to be circulated out during killing operations, measures have to be taken to
            treat the returned acid properly to avoid personnel from getting hurt, and without causing any environmental
         damage.

   13. Well Control Drills:

Drills shall be conducted only when hole conditions and other rig activities permit. Drills should be scheduled so that crews
can practice drills while on bottom, while tripping in and while out of the hole. Each operating shift shall perform at least one
BOP drill under different situations per month.

   14. Basic Requirement of Well Control:
               For 13-5/8”, 10K BOP, BOP should be schemed as follows: upper rams 5”, middle rams are shear rams,
         lower rams is variable rams (2-7/8” to 5”). For easily connecting, armored fire-resistant 10000psi and ID 4-1/16"
           flexible hoses are recommended as kill/choke lines which connected with BOP directly.
             The BOP stack and choke manifold, kill line and choke line, HCR and manual gate valves, inside BOP
          (including kelly cock) have to be H2S trimmed and stand the working temperature from -10 degrees C (in case of
         doing N2 for DST) to the maximum up to 100 deg C (estimated highest temperature condition).
             The BOP equipment shall be visually inspected, function tested, and hydrostatically pressure tested
          during commissioning and Acceptance of Drilling Unit prior to Commencement Date, then upon installation on a
          wellhead, prior to drilling any hole section with a potential hydrocarbon bearing reservoir, after breaking any BOP
          connection (such as after installation of an additional wellhead section after running casing), and at least every 14-
         21 days while the BOP is installed on the Well.
           CONTRACTOR  shall submit the current well control procedures manual of CONTRACTOR  to
      COMPANY for review and approval by COMPANY. Valid IWCF Well Control Certificates (or IADC equivalent)
         must be held by all tool pushers, Drillers (optional) at Drilling Supervisor level and Drillers and Assistant Drillers
            at Driller level, as further specified in CONTRACTOR Personnel HSE Requirements.
             The accumulator must be of sufficient capacity so that it is possible to close one (1) ram and the bag,
         open a ram and the bag, re-close a ram and the bag and retain 1200psi accumulator pressure.

────────────────────────────────────────────
────────────────────────────────────────────
           South Azadegan Oilfield Project

   Contract No:          F – 20 Well Drilling Program                  Page 169 of 181

             The “OPEN” and “CLOSED” operating positions of BOP activators of the rams, annular, and remote
          actuated valves must be clearly identified on the BOP master control panel and remote panels, in English in
          addition to the native language of the majority of the senior drilling crew.
               Shear-ram BOP activator must have a flap-cover to ensure only pre-meditated and deliberated activation
         and to prevent accidental WORK.
                All lines and connections in the choke and kill manifold, standpipe manifold, choke and kill lines and
          high-pressure mud lines should be welded hard-piping with weld-on FMC Weco “hammer-lug” 1502 unions (or
         Western equivalent, such as Anson) or weld-on API flanges. The well control system should not include any
          threaded screw-on hammer-lug unions, flanges, or other threaded screw-on connections.
                All adapter or spacer spools between the wellhead and BOP must be manufactured in accordance with
        API 6A, and must be complete with all required studs, nuts, and ring gasket seals for both lower and upper
          connections; minimum spare parts should include two (2) OEM spare studs complete with nuts and two spare OEM
           ring gasket seals for both bottom and top connections.
              There should be a wide-bore gas vent line from the “poor-boy” degasser; the line and line outlet must be
          securely anchored, the line outlet must be a safe distance from the well cellar, rig floor, choke manifold, and mud
          logging unit.
              Well Control Equipment Welding: All welds in the well control system must be NDT inspected and
          records maintained of all welding and weld inspections, including choke and kill manifold, standpipe manifold,
       mud pump discharge manifold, choke and kill lines.
              Well Control Rig Floor Information: The following must be posted on the rig floor (in the Driller’s shack
               / “dog-house”): Well Shut-In Procedure, Well Information Sheet (showing casing and hole sizes, depths, and such
            like), Kill Sheet (including most recent slow circulation rate pressures, formation integrity leak-off test information
         and MAASP, present mud weight, present drill string and well volumes, and such like).
             The derrick substructure clear height (rotary table to ground level) needs to be enough for fitting in
         above BOP configuration (including a tubing head and a bell nipple). This need to be double checked in rig tender
           process. According to the calculation of height of BOP stack, the substructure clear height is at least 9 m.
                In the case if drilling 6-1/8" hole becomes necessary, or using 3-1/2" drill pipe for the cleanup run
          (including drill 7" shoe track), then taper drilling string (5" DP + 3-1/2" DP) is to be run, so a pair variable ram be
           installed in the single ram preventer. The defect installing variable ram in single ram preventer is that the ram may
         be damaged and the string fall in hole bottom due to the ram hanging up too much weight when the drill pipe be
            cut. If the pipe ram be installed in the single ram preventer and the variable ram be installed in the double ram
           preventer, it would be very dangerous due to the variable ram failure in frequently using.

 12.9   H2S Monitoring and Preventing Measure

As per PPL/PEDEC SOW South Azadegan oilfield contains H2S bearing formations, precautions shall be
taken in place especially in 12-1/4” & 8-1/2” hole section (for typical Fahliyan vertical well) while
drilling, completion and well testing operation.
H2S can be released any time while drilling, completion and well testing operation, therefore:
    The Rig shall be prepared for H2S (Training, procedures and protective gear).
    Make sure everyone on the Rig should be made aware of the hazard presented by H2S during
            their induction when they first arrive on the rig.
    The mud shall be treated to protect the drill pipe and the casing.

────────────────────────────────────────────
────────────────────────────────────────────
          South Azadegan Oilfield Project

Contract No:          F – 20 Well Drilling Program                  Page 170 of 181

   All training program shall be recorded and reported.
   H2S emergency drill must be conducted frequently.
   Adequate sensors and alarms must be installed for the scope of monitoring the H2S releases.

────────────────────────────────────────────
────────────────────────────────────────────
            South Azadegan Oilfield Project
